ở lớp scheduler/task code trong slice kế tiếp.`n`n# 2026-05-23 | Fix missing historical OHLCV rendering in SaaS Stock Detail ✅`n`n## Problem Solved`n`nSaaS Stock Detail page (`packages/web`) did not The browser banner showed `Core API error 404` because the running container image was stale and the startup env did not forward `FRONTEND_URL` into the core services.`n`n## Verification Results`n`n| Check | Result |`n|---|---|`n| Backend route test | ✅ `user_workspace.test_portfolio_analytics_native_routes` passed locally |`n| Compose render | ✅ `docker compose -f docker-compose.ghcr.yml config --services` succeeded |`n| Core image rebuild | ✅ `ghcr.io/vietdungiitb/vninvest-core-api:20260510-enterprise` rebuilt successfully |`n| Server env fix | ✅ `FRONTEND_URL=https://vninvest.edusuccess.vn` added and forwarded to core-api/core-worker/core-beat |`n| Core service health | ✅ `vninvest-core-api` healthy after recreate |`n| Live API smoke | ✅ `/core/v1/workspace/portfolios/1/holdings|summary|analytics|technicals/` now return `200` |`n| Browser smoke | ✅ Logged-in dashboard loads without the `Core API error 404` banner |`n`n## Modified Files`n`n| File | Change |`n|------|--------|`n| `docker-compose.ghcr.yml` | Forwarded `FRONTEND_URL` into core-api, core-worker, and core-beat; documented the required var |`n| `compose.prod.env.example` | Added `FRONTEND_URL` to the production env template |`n| `CODE_CHANGE_AUDIT_LEDGER.md` | Added this audit ledger entry |`n| `PROGRESS_DASHBOARD.md` | Added matching progress snapshot for the core-api redeploy slice |`n| `MODIFIED_FILES_AUDIT_T12_PHASE_4_20250501.md` | Added centralized modified-file entry |`n`n## Residual Risk`n`n- Browser auth sessions can still expire or get revoked after backend restarts; the API itself is now healthy and the native routes respond correctly once authenticated.`n`n# 2026-05-22 | Frontend image redeploy for /admin-ops SOC cutover ✅`n`n## Problem Solved`n`nProduction frontend was still serving the stale legacy bundle at `/admin-ops`, so the user kept seeing the old admin shell. Those are outside this route-cutover slice and were not changed here.`n`n# 2026-05-22 | Audit remediation — Stock Detail SaaS Native VNStockChart Redesign ✅`n`n## Problem Solved`n`nSau audit ngày 2026-05-22 phát hiện 2 FAIL + 1 design-intent gap + 1 CSS debt trong codebase stock-detail SaaS Native, đội code đã sửa hoàn toàn 100%:`n`n- **FAIL-1 NGHIÊM TRỌNG**: 6 dòng hardcoded fallback data (`|| 0.45`, `|| 0.35`, `?? 1250000`, `?? 980000`, `?? 45000000000`) trong `StockContextDeck.js` đã được xóa. Khi không có dữ liệu, UI hiển thị empty-state trung thực thay vì con số bịa.`n- **FAIL-2 TRUNG BÌNH**: 2 stub `window.alert()` trong `StockDetailPage.js` đã được thay bằng Toast Notification in-page với hiệu ứng fade-in, tự ẩn sau 3 giây. CSS toast đã được bổ sung vào `StockDetailPage.css`.`n- **Design Intent Gap**: Thêm comment kiến trúc trong `OrderFlowRadarPanel.js` giải thích rõ deliberate design tradeoff: CVD intraday luôn là today's data; cross-day session-date sync được hoãn sang lane SD-06.`n- **CSS Debt**: Xóa hoàn toàn `.sdp-content-layout` và `.sdp-side-column` — 2 class dead code không được dùng trong JS.`n`n## Verification Results`n`n| Check | Result |`n|---|---|`n| `node scripts/check-stock-detail-shell.mjs` | ✅ `core-stock-detail shell wiring OK` |`n| `hardcoded 0.45 / 0.35 / 1250000 / 980000 / 45000000000` | ✅ ALL REMOVED |`n| `window.alert()` in StockDetailPage | ✅ REMOVED — replaced by Toast |`n| Toast/notification mechanism present | ✅ YES |`n| Toast CSS present | ✅ YES |`n| Design intent comment in OrderFlowRadarPanel | ✅ YES |`n| `.sdp-content-layout` dead CSS removed | ✅ YES |`n| `.sdp-side-column` dead CSS removed | ✅ YES |`n| StockContextDeck has honest empty state | ✅ YES |`n| Adapter test count | ✅ 42 tests PASS |`n`n## Modified Files`n`n| File | Change |`n|------|--------|`n| `packages/web/src/features/stock-detail/components/StockContextDeck.js` | Xóa 6 dòng hardcoded fallback — hiển thị empty state trung thực |`n| `packages/web/src/pages/StockDetailPage.js` | Thay `window.alert()` bằng Toast Notification in-page |`n| `packages/web/src/pages/StockDetailPage.css` | Thêm toast CSS, xóa `.sdp-content-layout` và `.sdp-side-column` |`n| `packages/web/src/components/OrderFlowRadarPanel.js` | Thêm design intent comment cho session-date tradeoff |`n| `CODE_CHANGE_AUDIT_LEDGER.md` | Entry này |`n| `PROGRESS_DASHBOARD.md` | Cập nhật progress snapshot |`n`n## Residual Risk`n`n- Tất cả FAIL và debt đã được khắc phục. Không còn residual risk từ audit ngày 2026-05-22.`n- `OrderFlowRadarPanel` cross-day session sync vẫn là deferred work trong lane SD-06 — đã được ghi nhận tường minh trong comment code.`n`n---`n`n# 2026-05-22 | Rebased SaaS architecture cutover roadmap to audited codebase reality ✅`n`n## Problem Solved`n`n`docs/SAAS_ARCHITECTURE_AUDIT_AND_CUTOVER_ROADMAP_20260522.md` trước đó phản ánh chưa sát hiện trạng code. Nếu dùng nguyên bản cũ để lập kế hoạch cutover thì rất dễ ưu tiên sai.`n`nKey outcomes:`n1. Rebase lại kiến trúc hiện trạng theo snapshot đã audit từ source code.`n2. WOW Aesthetics (Glassmorphic):** Nhúng động stylesheet CSS với thiết kế tối cao cấp, các viền neon phát sáng theo mức độ nghiêm trọng (severity), hiệu ứng kính mờ (glassmorphic blur), và các micro-animations chuyển tiếp mượt mà.`n- **Module 1 (Real-time Alert Statistics & Feed):** Hiển thị danh sách cảnh báo giao dịch dòng tiền lớn (Whale, Dark Flow, Price Shock, Stale Quote) kèm panel hiển thị bằng chứng (evidence details) dạng rút gọn/mở rộng, drawer pháp y (drawer detail context), bộ lọc thời gian thực và tự động làm mới (auto-refresh 5s) với đèn pulse nhấp nháy.`n- **Module 2 (Statistics by Stock Symbol):** Thống kê và gom nhóm cảnh báo tự động theo mã cổ phiếu, tính toán tần suất xuất hiện tương đối (Bùng nổ, Cao, Trung bình, Thấp), tích hợp biểu đồ thanh cường độ Mua/Bán (Buy/Sell Strength Meter) cực kỳ trực quan, lọc nhanh theo mã và liên kết trực tiếp tới Stock detail workspace `/stocks/:symbol`.`n- **Vietnamese i18n & Parity:** Toàn bộ văn bản hiển thị sử dụng tiếng Việt chuẩn hóa đầy đủ dấu, đồng thời bảo toàn 100% mã nguồn immutable bubble chart từ dashboard cũ để tránh gây regression.`n`n## Modified Files`n`n| File | Change |`n|------|--------|`n| hardening + native SaaS ownership closure ✅`n`n## Problem Solved`n`nSlice này chốt dứt điểm native core ownership cho partner PAT/service-account path trong `packages/core/backend` thay vì vá legacy backend, đồng thời khóa toàn bộ nhóm auth gap đã lộ ra qua nhiều vòng review.`n`nPhần hardening đã hoàn tất trong slice này:`n`n- Giữ route owner ở native core path cho `/core/v1/partner/*`; không sửa runtime qua `backend/portfolio/*`.`n- Bổ sung contract native cho service account và PAT theo shape frontend đang dùng: `id`, `environment`, `policy_ids`, `object`, `scope_codes`, `secret`, `new_token`.`n- Thêm trạng thái `ROTATING` và grace window cho rotate, vẫn cho token cũ sống trong cửa sổ grace theo thiết kế.`n- Chuyển human login PAT từ heuristic mutable (`label`, `audit_reason`) sang provenance bit bất biến `is_human_session`.`n- Thêm migration revoke toàn bộ PAT legacy giả dạng login session để chặn bypass còn sót trong DB cũ.`n- Thêm bounded TTL cho human session PAT và refresh rotation thật: refresh chỉ dành cho human-session PAT, cấp secret mới, revoke secret cũ.`n- Chặn PAT của service account đã `disabled` ở auth path, issue path, rotate path, và refresh path.`n- Chặn tenant tự lockout bằng cách disable/xóa service account active cuối cùng.`n- Canonicalize `policy_ids -> scope_codes` cho service account/PAT path và migrate dữ liệu cũ sang canonical scope vocabulary.`n- Tước quyền `is_staff` / `is_superuser` khỏi machine PAT principal ở auth layer để machine PAT không bleed sang operator surfaces.`n- Áp reusable PAT/human-session permission guard lên các endpoint identity-access liền kề (`partner billing/usage`, `DNSE credential`, `tenant members`, `user profile`, `user config`) để low-scope machine PAT không còn đi xuyên qua các route chỉ dùng `IsAuthenticated`.`n`n## Modified Files`n`n| File | Change |`n|------|--------|`n| `packages/core/backend/src/identity_access/models.py` | Added immutable `is_human_session` flag and issuance support `86400s` thay vì fallback `600s`.`n- Thêm regression test xác nhận một run thành công cách hiện tại 6 giờ vẫn được xem là healthy cho daily cadence.`n- Thêm regression test xác nhận khi job daily thực apply `@throttle_classes([AuthBootstrapThrottle])` to `profile_view` and `capabilities_view` | SOC-NAV-FIX |`n| `packages/core/backend/src/vninvest_core/settings.py` | ADD `'auth_bootstrap': '60/min'` to `DEFAULT_THROTTLE_RATES` | SOC-NAV-FIX |`n| `frontend/src/components/TopNav.jsx` | FIX `canReadSOC` line 71: add `\|\| Boolean(userIdentity?.isStaff)` fallback so SOC nav survives transient throttle failures for staff users | SOC-NAV-FIX |`n`n## Root Cause`n`nGlobal `user` throttle bucket (1000/hour) was exhausted by realtime market-data polling (~720–1200 req/hour). `/auth/me/` and `/api/core/v1/access/capabilities/` share this bucket, so both returned 429 simultaneously → CASL `ability` stayed empty → `ability.can('read','ServiceOpsCenter')` = false → SOC nav item hidden.`n`n## Fix Summary`n`n- **Backend**: `AuthBootstrapThrottle` isolates auth bootstrap endpoints into their own Redis key (`auth_bootstrap: 60/min`), completely separate from the `user` bucket. Realtime polling can never exhaust this new bucket.`n- **Frontend**: `|| Boolean(userIdentity?.isStaff)` defensive fallback — if ability is empty (throttle outage), staff users still see SOC nav.`n`n## Validation`n`n- `AuthBootstrapThrottle.scope = 'auth_bootstrap'` confirmed in running container`n- `capabilities_view.cls.throttle_classes = [AuthBootstrapThrottle]` confirmed`n- `profile_view.cls.throttle_classes = [AuthBootstrapThrottle]` confirmed`n- All 14 containers healthy after rebuild`n- Commit: pushed to `origin/dev``n`n---`n`n## Modified / Created Files`n`n| File | Change | Slice |`n|------|--------|-------|`n| `packages/core/backend/src/identity_access/views_auth.py` | ADD `AuthBootstrapThrottle(UserRateThrottle)` class with `scope='auth_bootstrap'`; apply `@throttle_classes([AuthBootstrapThrottle])` to `profile_view` and `capabilities_view` | SOC-NAV-FIX |`n| `packages/core/backend/src/vninvest_core/settings.py` | ADD `'auth_bootstrap': '60/min'` to `DEFAULT_THROTTLE_RATES` | SOC-NAV-FIX |`n| `frontend/src/components/TopNav.jsx` | FIX `canReadSOC` line 71: add `\|\| Boolean(userIdentity?.isStaff)` fallback so SOC nav survives transient throttle failures for staff users | SOC-NAV-FIX |`n`n## Root Cause`n`nGlobal `user` throttle bucket (1000/hour) was exhausted by realtime market-data polling (~720–1200 req/hour). `/auth/me/` and `/api/core/v1/access/capabilities/` share this bucket, so both returned 429 simultaneously → CASL `ability` stayed empty → `ability.can('read','ServiceOpsCenter')` = false → SOC nav item hidden.`n`n## Fix Summary`n`n- **Backend**: `AuthBootstrapThrottle` isolates auth bootstrap endpoints into their own Redis key (`auth_bootstrap: 60/min`), completely separate from the `user` bucket. Realtime polling can never exhaust this new bucket.`n- **Frontend**: `|| Boolean(userIdentity?.isStaff)` defensive fallback — if ability is empty (throttle outage), staff users still see SOC nav.`n`n## Validation`n`n- `AuthBootstrapThrottle.scope = 'auth_bootstrap'` confirmed in running container`n- `capabilities_view.cls.throttle_classes = [AuthBootstrapThrottle]` confirmed`n- `profile_view.cls.throttle_classes = [AuthBootstrapThrottle]` confirmed`n- All 14 containers healthy after rebuild`n- Commit: pushed to `origin/dev``n`n---`n`n# 2026-05-19 | B01 Scheduler Run Truth Hardening — Complete`n`n## Modified / Created Files`n`n| File | Change | Slice |`n|------|--------|-------|`n| `packages/core/backend/src/service_ops/scheduler_catalog.py` | ADD `service_key: 'backfill_daily_prices'` to `daily-backfill-prices-1700`; | dev stack fix`n`n## Problem`nFrontend showed:`n- Heatmap: 0% across all indicators (CVD, Buy/Sell Strength)`n- Whale Alert Center: 0 alerts, completely empty`n- Watchlist data: OK (populated normally)`n`nRoot cause: `WHALE_ALERT_DETECTION_ENABLED` was NOT set in `backend/.env.dev` → Celery beat task `detect-global-whale-alerts` never scheduled → `WhaleAlertEvent` table remained empty → API returned no whale alerts.`n`n## Solution Applied`n**File Changed:** `backend/.env.dev` (not committed; local config)`n`n````n# Whale alert detection (populate WhaleAlertEvent table)`nWHALE_ALERT_DETECTION_ENABLED=True`nWHALE_ALERT_DETECTION_INTERVAL_SECONDS=120`n````n`n- Enables Celery beat task to run every 120 seconds`n- Task `portfolio.tasks.detect_and_store_global_whale_alerts` populates `WhaleAlertEvent` table`n- API endpoints `/realtime/whale-feed/aggregate/` now have data source`n`n## Validation Steps`n1. Restarted dev stack with `./start_dev.ps1` to pick up new env settings`n2. Celery beat should now schedule task`n3. After ~120-180 seconds, `WhaleAlertEvent.objects.count()` > 0`n4. Frontend whale-feed and heatmap endpoints should return live data`n`n## Notes`n- `.env.dev` is gitignored (correct for environment files)`n- Fix is per-dev-environment; production has this already 201 Created: register_view (new user created)`n- 200 OK: password_reset_request_view (request successful)`n- 200 OK: password_reset_confirm_view (token verified)`n- 400 Bad Request: missing/invalid fields, short password`n- 409 Conflict: duplicate username/email`n`n✅ **R4 (Standard Error Envelope):**`n- All errors now returned in StandardErrorEnvelope format`n- Fields: detail, code, timestamp (ISO8601), request_id (req_* prefix), field_errors (optional)`n- Implemented via custom exception handler in DRF integration`n`n✅ **R5 (No Swallow Exceptions):**`n- No changes to exception handling patterns from Phase 1; C2+C5 fixes remain effective`n`n### Changes Breakdown`n`n**Error Handling Architecture:**`n````nView raises exception`n  ↓`ncore_api_exception_handler() catches`n  ↓`nConverts to StandardErrorEnvelope`n  ↓`nReturns DRF Response with correct status code`n````n`n**Example: register_view with A4-A5**`n```python`n# Before (incorrect):`nif User.objects.filter(username=username).exists():`n    return Response({'detail': 'User exists'}, status=409)`n`n# After (A4-A5 compliant):`nif User.objects.filter(username=username).exists():`n    raise ConflictErrorException(detail='Username already taken.')`n    # Handler returns:`n    # {`n    #   "detail": "Username already taken.",`n    #   "code": "conflict_error",`n    #   "timestamp": "2026-05-13T15:30:00Z",`n    #   "request_id": "req_abc123xyz"`n    # }`n````n`n### Risk Assessment`n`n**Residual Risks:** NONE`n- All auth endpoints tested with standard envelope`n- Status code tests`n`n---`n`n**Slice 1 Validation Complete.** Proceeding to Slice 2.`n`n---`n`n# 2026-05-22 | Production deploy to docker.edusuccess.vn ✅`n`n## Problem Solved`n`nProduction deploy for VNInvest core-only stack was blocked by two runtime issues during server bring-up:`n`n- GHCR pull on the server was denied for the private package-backed images.`n- `core-worker` and `core-beat` crashed on startup because `CSRF_TRUSTED_ORIGINS` was missing from the GHCR compose env.`n`nResolved by:`n`n1. Deploying with the already cached GHCR-tagged images available on the host: `20260510-enterprise`.`n2. Adding `CSRF_TRUSTED_ORIGINS` to the production compose env for `core-api`, `core-worker`, and `core-beat`.`n3. Persisting `APP_RELEASE=20260510-enterprise` in the server production env so the stack can restart without pulling.`n4. Recreating `core-worker` and `core-beat` after the env fix.`n`n## Validation Results`n`n| Check | Result |`n|---|---|`n| `git diff --check -- docker-compose.ghcr.yml` | ✅ clean |`n| `docker compose -f docker-compose.ghcr.yml --env-file .env.production up -d --pull missing --force-recreate` | ✅ stack reconciled |`n| `docker compose -f docker-compose.ghcr.yml --env-file .env.production ps` | ✅ `core-api`, `core-worker`, `core-beat`, `frontend` up |`n| `curl -4 -k -sS https://vninvest.edusuccess.vn/core/v1/health` | ✅ `status: ok` |`n| `curl -4 -k -I https://vninvest.edusuccess.vn` | ✅ `HTTP/2 200` |`n`n## Modified Files`n`n| File | Change |`n|------|--------|`n| `docker-compose.ghcr.yml` | Added `CSRF_TRUSTED_ORIGINS` to `core-api`, `core-worker`, and `core-beat` env blocks |`n| `CODE_CHANGE_AUDIT_LEDGER.md` | Added this deployment entry |`n| `PROGRESS_DASHBOARD.md` | Added matching progress snapshot |`n`n## Residual Risk`n`n- Fresh GHCR publish is still pending private registry credentials; production is currently running on the cached GHCR-tagged images already present on the host.`n- The running stack is healthy and serving HTTPS, but the registry auth gap remains if you want new images pushed upstream.`n`n---`n`n## [2026-05-26] DNSE Backfill Rate-Limit Detection + Retry — commit `389daebb``n`n### Problem`n`n`fetch_intraday_tape_history` silently swallowed Rate Limit Exceeded from vnbroker library (which logs a WARNING and returns empty DataFrame instead of raising). Result: task returned `status: 'empty'` for both "no data" and "rate limited" cases — impossible to distinguish, no retry.`n`n### Fix`n`n**`packages/core/backend/src/market_data/vnbroker_adapter.py`**`n- Add `DnseRateLimitError(VnbrokerAdapterError)` exception class`n- Add `_RateLimitWatcher(logging.Handler)` that intercepts vnbroker WARNING logs containing 'rate limit' during `q.intraday()` call`n- Raise `DnseRateLimitError` when rate limit detected (propagate, not swallow)`n- Add `except DnseRateLimitError: raise` before general `except Exception:` catch`n`n**`packages/core/backend/src/data_ingest/tasks.py`**`n- Import `DnseRateLimitError` alongside `fetch_intraday_tape_history``n- Wrap fetch call in `try/except DnseRateLimitError``n- On rate limit: `self.retry(countdown=300×2^retries, max_retries=3)` → retries after 5min, 10min, 20min`n`n**`packages/core/backend/src/data_ingest/tests/test_backfill_tasks.py`**`n- T-C8: `_RateLimitWatcher` triggers on 'rate limit' warning`n- T-C8b: `_RateLimitWatcher` ignores other warnings`n- T-C9: `fetch_intraday_tape_history` raises `DnseRateLimitError` when vnbroker logs rate limit`n- T-C9b: No rate limit warning → returns `[]` normally`n`n### Validation`n`n| Check | Result |`n|---|---|`n| `manage.py test test_backfill_tasks` | ✅ 18/18 PASS |`n| `DnseRateLimitError` import in worker | ✅ OK |`n| `from market_data.vnbroker_adapter import DnseRateLimitError` | ✅ OK |`n`n### Status`n`n- DNSE server-side rate limit still active at time of commit (from 76K burst earlier today)`n- **Action required**: once DNSE rate limit resets, re-trigger focused backfill:`n  ```powershell`n  docker exec vninvest-dev-core-api python /app/src/manage.py shell -c "`n  from data_ingest.tasks import backfill_intraday_tick_system`n  from market_data.models import IntradayTick`n  import datetime`n`n  LARGE_CAP = ['HPG','VNM','VCB','BID','CTG','TCB','VIC','VHM','MSN','MWG',`n               'GAS','VJC','HDB','SSI','ACB','STB','VPB','MBB','FPT']`n  today = datetime.date.today()`n  dates = []`n  cur = today - datetime.timedelta(days=90)`n  while cur <= today:`n      if cur.weekday() < 5: dates.append(cur.isoformat())`n      cur += datetime.timedelta(days=1)`n`n  existing = set()`n  for sym, dt in IntradayTick.objects.filter(symbol__in=LARGE_CAP).values_list('symbol','timestamp__date').distinct():`n      existing.add((sym, str(dt)))`n`n  queued = 0`n  for sym in LARGE_CAP:`n      for dt in dates:`n          if (sym, dt) not in existing:`n              backfill_intraday_tick_system.apply_async(`n                  kwargs={'symbol': sym, 'trade_date': dt},`n                  queue='manual_on_demand', countdown=queued * 3)`n              queued += 1`n  print(f'Queued {queued} tasks, ETA ~{queued*3/60:.1f}min')`n  "`n  ````n`n### Modified Files`n`n| File | Change |`n|------|--------|`n| `packages/core/backend/src/market_data/vnbroker_adapter.py` | `DnseRateLimitError` + `_RateLimitWatcher` + propagate rate limit |`n| T-C8/C8b/C9/C9b |`n| `CODE_CHANGE_AUDIT_LEDGER.md` | This entry |`n`n---`n`n## 2026-05-27 | Redis Hostname Drift Guardrail (Dev + Production)`n`n### Context`n`n- Incident symptom: heatmap all `0.00%`, Health Compat Route + Runtime Hotfix`n`n### Context`n`n- Public endpoint `https://vninvest.edusuccess.vn/api/health/` returned `404` while canonical core endpoint `/core/v1/health` was healthy.`n- This broke legacy probes expecting `/api/health/` compatibility.`n`n### Root Cause`n`n- Frontend nginx routed `/api/*` correctly to `core-api`, but `core-api` URLConf did not expose `/api/health/`.`n`n### Fix Applied (Source)`n`n- Added compatibility routes in core backend URLConf:`n   - `path('api/health', views_health.health, ...)``n   - `path('api/health/', views_health.health, ...)``n- Added explicit frontend nginx compatibility locations:`n   - `location = /api/health` -> proxy to `/core/v1/health``n   - `location = /api/health/` -> proxy to `/core/v1/health``n- Added regression test `test_api_health_compat_route_returns_ok` in runtime baseline tests.`n- Regenerated `module_tree_full.md`.`n`n### Runtime Hotfix Applied (Production)`n`n- Uploaded updated nginx config to server and hot-reloaded frontend container:`n   - `docker cp /home/pham/nginx.conf.hotfix vninvest-frontend:/etc/nginx/conf.d/default.conf``n   - `docker exec vninvest-frontend nginx -t && docker exec vninvest-frontend nginx -s reload``n- Result: both compat endpoints now return `200` with health JSON payload.`n`n### Validation`n`n| Check | Result |`n|---|---|`n| `curl -k https://vninvest.edusuccess.vn/api/health/` | ✅ 200 |`n| `curl -k https://vninvest.edusuccess.vn/api/health` | ✅ 200 |`n| `curl -k https://vninvest.edusuccess.vn/core/v1/health` | ✅ 200 |`n| `python scripts/ops/generate_module_tree.py` | ✅ PASS |`n| `python -m pytest packages/core/backend/tests/test_runtime_baseline.py -q` | ⚠️ blocked locally (`ModuleNotFoundError: dj_database_url`) |`n`n### Residual Risk`n`n- Runtime hotfix in running frontend container is effective immediately but should be considered non-durable until normal image redeploy picks up committed nginx source changes.`n`n### Modified Files`n`n| File | Change |`n|------|--------|`n| `packages/core/backend/src/vninvest_core/urls.py` | Added `/api/health`
